Appendix B
Convergence of Fed-Norm-SGDA for Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Functions (
Theorem

1
)

We organize this section as follows. First, in
Section

B.1
we present some intermediate results, which we use to prove the main theorem. Next, in
Section

B.2
, we present the proof of
Theorem

1
, which is followed by the proofs of the intermediate results in
Section

B.3
.
Section

B.4
contains some auxiliary results. Finally, in
Section

B.5
we discuss the convergence result for nonconvex-PL functions.

The problem we solve is

\displaystyle\min_{{\mathbf{x}}}\max_{{\mathbf{y}}}\left\{{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\right\}.

We define
{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\max_{{\mathbf{y}}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
and
{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\in\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
. Since
{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
is
\mu
-strongly concave,
{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})
is unique.
In Fed-Norm-SGDA (
Algorithm

1
), the client updates are given by

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}={\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-\eta_{x}^{c}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}),

(12)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}={\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}+\eta_{y}^{c}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}),

where
1\leq k\leq\tau_{i}
. These client updates are then aggregated to compute
\{{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}},{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\}

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right);\quad{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right)

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right);\quad{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right).

Remark 7
.

Note that we have made explicit, the dependence on
k
in
a_{i}^{(j)}(k)
above. This was omitted in the main paper to avoid tedious notation. However, for some local optimizers, such as local momentum at the clients (
Section

A.1
), the coefficients
a_{i}^{(j)}(k)
change with
k
. We assume in our subsequent analysis that
a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\leq\alpha
for all
j\in\{0,1,\dots,k-1\}
and for all
k\in\{1,2,\dots,\tau_{i}\}
. Further, we assume that
\big\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(k)\big\|_{1}\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(k)\big\|_{1}
and
\big\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(k+1)\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(k+1)\big\|_{2}
for feasible
k
. We also use the notation
{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}\triangleq\big\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}(\tau_{i})\big\|
.

At iteration
t
, the server samples
|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|
clients
without
replacement
(WOR)
uniformly at random. While aggregating at the server, client
i
update is weighed by
\tilde{w}_{i}=w_{i}n/|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|
. The aggregates
({{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}},{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}})
computed at the server are of the form

\displaystyle{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}},\quad\text{such that}\quad\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}[{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}]=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\Big]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}

(13)

\displaystyle{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}},\quad\text{such that}\quad\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}[{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}]=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\Big]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}

For simplicity of analysis, unless stated otherwise, we assume that
|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|=P
for all
t
.
Finally, server updates the
{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}
variables as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}={\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}},\qquad{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}={\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}.

B.1
Intermediate Lemmas

We begin with the following result from
Nouiehed et al. 2019
about the smoothness of
{\widetilde{\Phi}}(\cdot)
.

Lemma B.1
.

If a function
f(\cdot,\cdot)
satisfies Assumptions
1
,
4
(
L_{f}
-smoothness and
\mu
-strong concavity in
{\mathbf{y}}
), then
\phi(\cdot)\triangleq\max_{\mathbf{y}}f(\cdot,{\mathbf{y}})
is
L_{\Phi}
-smooth with
L_{\Phi}=\kappa L_{f}/2+L_{f}
, where
\kappa=L_{f}/\mu
is the condition number.

Lemma B.2
.

Suppose the local client loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
and the stochastic oracles for the local functions satisfy
2
.
Suppose the server selects
P
clients in each round. Then the iterates generated by Fed-Norm-SGDA (
Algorithm

1
) satisfy

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(14)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{n(n-P)}{n-1}\left[\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{2}{P}\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right],

where,
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\|^{2}+\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\|^{2}\right]
is the iterate drift for client
i
, at local iteration
k
in the
t
-th communication round.

Lemma B.3
.

Suppose the local client loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
4
,
and the stochastic oracles for the local functions satisfy
2
.
Also, the server learning rate
\gamma_{x}^{s}
satisfies
64\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\beta_{L}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{n}{P}(\max_{i}w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}/{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2})\leq 1
,
8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\right)\max\{8\beta_{G}^{2},1\}\leq 1
, and
8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\beta_{L}^{2}\frac{n}{P}(\max_{i,k}w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})/{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1})\leq 1
. Then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
satisfy

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right]\leq-\frac{7\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{16}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(15)

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{4}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+\frac{9\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{4\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(2(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n-P}{n-1}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right].

Remark
.

The bound in
Equation

15
looks very similar to the corresponding one-step decay bound for simple smooth minimization problems. The major difference is the presence of
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
, which quantifies the inaccuracy of
{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}
in solving the
max
problem
\max_{\mathbf{y}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}})
.
The term
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)
is the client drift and is bounded in
Lemma

B.4
.

Lemma B.4
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
4
,
and the stochastic oracles for the local functions satisfy
2
.
Further, in
Algorithm

1
, we choose learning rates
\eta_{x}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}
such that
\max\{\eta_{x}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}\}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{f}(\max_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1})\sqrt{2(1+\beta_{L}^{2})}}
.
Then, the iterates
\{{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t)}}\}
generated by Fed-Norm-SGDA (
Algorithm

1
) satisfy

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\leq 2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\sigma_{G}^{2}

\displaystyle+8L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+8L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right],

where
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.

Lemma B.5
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
4
, and the stochastic oracles for the local functions satisfy
2
.
The server learning rates
\gamma_{x}^{s},\gamma_{y}^{s}
satisfy the following conditions:

2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\leq 1,\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\kappa L_{f}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{n}{P}\max\left\{\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}},\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{64},\gamma_{x}^{s}\leq\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}}{156\kappa^{2}},

8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\beta_{L}^{2}\frac{n}{P}\max\left\{\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}},\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right\}\leq 1,\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\beta_{G}\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\max\left\{\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i},\beta_{L}\sqrt{\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{40\kappa}

The client learning rates
\eta_{x}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}
satisfy
\eta_{y}^{c}L_{f}\beta_{G}\leq\frac{1}{16\sqrt{\kappa M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
and
\eta_{x}^{c}
:
\eta_{x}^{c}L_{f}\beta_{G}\leq\frac{1}{64\kappa\sqrt{M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
, respectively.
Then the iterates generated by Fed-Norm-SGDA (
Algorithm

1
) satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

(16)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)},{\mathbf{y}}^{(0)})\right]}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu T}+\frac{1}{12\mu\kappa^{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{8\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\kappa\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+18\kappa L_{f}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\kappa}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right].

B.2
Proof of
Theorem

1

For the sake of completeness, we first state the full statement of
Theorem

1
here.

Theorem
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}_{i}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
.
Suppose the server selects clients using without-replacement sampling scheme
(WOR)
.
Also, the server learning rates
\gamma_{x}^{s},\gamma_{y}^{s}
and the client learning rates
\eta_{x}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}
satisfy the conditions specified in
Lemma

B.5
.
Then the iterates generated by Fed-Norm-SGDA (
Algorithm

1
) satisfy

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[0:T-1]}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\left[\frac{\Delta_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}T}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}}{P}\left(A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right]\right)}_{\text{Error with full synchronization}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]\right)}_{\text{Error due to local updates}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\frac{n-P}{n-1}\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}E_{w}\tau_{\text{eff}}\sigma_{G}^{2}}{P}\right)}_{\text{Partial Participation Error}},

where
\kappa=L_{f}/\mu
is the condition number,
{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\max_{\mathbf{y}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
is the envelope function,

\Delta_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}\triangleq{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)})-\min_{\mathbf{x}}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})
,

A_{w}\triangleq n\tau_{\text{eff}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}
,

B_{w}\triangleq n\tau_{\text{eff}}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}
,

C_{w}\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}({\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-[\alpha^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}]^{2})
,

D\triangleq\max_{i}(\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2})
, where
\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\triangleq[a_{i}^{(0)},a_{i}^{(1)},\dots,a_{i}^{(\tau_{i}-2)}]^{\top}
for all
i
and

E_{w}\triangleq n\max_{i}w_{i}
.

Using
\gamma_{y}^{s}=\Theta\big(\frac{1}{L_{f}}\sqrt{\frac{P}{\bar{\tau}T}}\big)
and
\eta_{x}^{c}\leq\eta_{y}^{c}=\Theta\big(\frac{1}{L_{f}\bar{\tau}\sqrt{T}}\big)
, where
\bar{\tau}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{i}
in the bounds above, we get

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\frac{(\bar{\tau}/\tau_{\text{eff}})+A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}}{\sqrt{P\bar{\tau}T}}\right)}_{\text{Error with full synchronization}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\frac{C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D\sigma_{G}^{2}}{\bar{\tau}^{2}T}\right)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Local updates error}\end{subarray}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}E_{w}\tau_{\text{eff}}\sigma_{G}^{2}}{\sqrt{P\bar{\tau}T}}\right)}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{Partial participation}\\
\text{error}\end{subarray}}.

Proof.

Using
Lemma

B.3
, and substituting in the bound on iterates’ drift from
Lemma

B.4
, we can bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right]\leq-\frac{7\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{16}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{9\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{4\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(2(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n-P}{n-1}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{5}{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+10\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+L_{f}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\right].

(17)

Summing (
17
) over
t=0,\ldots,T-1
, substituting the bound on
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
from
Lemma

B.5
, and rearranging the terms, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}T}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\kappa^{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left({\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-[a^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}]^{2}\right)+\sigma_{G}^{2}\max_{i}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right]\right)

(18)

Consequently, using constants
A_{w},B_{w},C_{w},D,E_{w}
, (
18
) can be simplified to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\kappa^{2}\left[\frac{\Delta_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}T}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}}{P}\left(A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+(B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}+E_{w}\tau_{\text{eff}})\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right]+\kappa^{2}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]\right).

which completes the proof.
∎

Convergence in terms of
F

Proof of
1.1
.

According to the definition of
F({\mathbf{x}})
and
\widetilde{F}({\mathbf{x}})
, we have

\displaystyle\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[p_{i}\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))-w_{i}\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))\right]

(
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\in\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}}F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}p_{i}\left[\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(p_{i}-w_{i}\right)\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))

\displaystyle=\left[\nabla_{x}F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla_{x}F({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{p_{i}-w_{i}}{\sqrt{w}_{i}}\cdot\sqrt{w}_{i}\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})).

Taking norm, using
L_{f}
-smoothness and applying Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})-{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(p_{i}-w_{i})^{2}}{w_{i}}\right]\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})-{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2\chi^{2}_{\mathbf{p}\|\mathbf{w}}\left[\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right],

where the last inequality uses
3
. Next, note that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left[2\chi^{2}_{\mathbf{p}\|\mathbf{w}}\beta_{G}^{2}+1\right]\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}+4\left[\chi^{2}_{\mathbf{p}\|\mathbf{w}}\sigma_{G}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=2\left[2\chi^{2}_{\mathbf{p}\|\mathbf{w}}\beta_{G}^{2}+1\right]\epsilon_{\text{opt}}+4\left[\chi^{2}_{\mathbf{p}\|\mathbf{w}}\sigma_{G}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right].

where
\epsilon_{\text{opt}}
denotes the optimization error in the right hand side of (
4
) in
Theorem

1
.
∎

Proof of
1.2
.

If clients are weighted equally (
w_{i}=p_{i}=1/n
for all
i
), with each carrying out
\tau
steps of local SGDA, as seen in (
6
) we get

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\left\|\nabla\Phi({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\localvar^2 + \varscale^2 \hetero^2}{\sqrt{\selclients\sync T}}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\localvar^2 + \sync\hetero^2}{\sync T}$}}+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\numclients- \selclients}{\numclients- 1}$}}\right)\sigma_{G}^{2}\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sync}{\selclients T}$}}}\Big).

•

For full client participation, this reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\tau T}}+\frac{1}{T}\right).

To reach an
\epsilon
-stationary point, assuming
n\tau\leq T
, the per-client gradient complexity is
T\tau=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{n\epsilon^{4}}\right)
. Since
\tau\leq T/n
, the minimum number of communication rounds required is
T=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)
.

•

For partial participation,
\mathcal{O}\Big(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\numclients- \selclients}{\numclients- 1}$}}\right)\sigma_{G}^{2}\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sync}{\selclients T}$}}}\Big)
is the dominant term, and we do not get any convergence benefit of multiple local updates. Consequently, per-gradient client complexity and number of communication rounds are both
T\tau=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{P\epsilon^{4}}\right)
, for
\tau=\mathcal{O}(1)
. However, if the data across clients comes from identical distributions (
\sigma_{G}=0
), then we recover
per-client gradient complexity of
\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{P\epsilon^{4}}\right)
, and number of communication rounds
=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)
.

∎

B.3
Proofs of the Intermediate Lemmas

Proof of
Lemma

B.2
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}\left({{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}+{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}\left({{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right)\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}^{2}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(sampling scheme)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(
\because\tilde{w}_{i}=w_{i}n/P
)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}.

(19)

where the last inequality follows from
1
and
2
.
Further, we can bound the second term as follows.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

((WOR) sampling)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}))^{2}\tilde{w}_{i}^{2}+w_{i}^{2}-2\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}w_{i}\right)\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left\langle(\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}-w_{i}){\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}},(\mathbb{I}(j\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{j}-w_{j}){\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},j}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[w_{i}^{2}\left(\frac{n}{P}-1\right)\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\cdot\mathbb{I}(j\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}\tilde{w}_{j}-\mathbb{I}(j\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{j}w_{i}-\mathbb{I}(i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)})\tilde{w}_{i}w_{j}+w_{i}w_{j}\right)\left\langle{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}},{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},j}}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{n}{P}-1\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[w_{i}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[w_{i}w_{j}\left(\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)-1\right)\left\langle{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}},{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},j}}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{n}{P}\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2},

(20)

Next, we bound the second term in (
20
).

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}-\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2(\max_{i}w_{i})\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right),

(21)

using
3
.
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\|^{2}+\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\|^{2}\right]
. Substituting (
21
) in (
20
), and using the resulting bound in (
19
) we get the bound in (
14
).
∎

Proof of
Lemma

B.3
.

Since the local functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy
4
,
F({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
is
\mu
-SC for any
{\mathbf{x}}
.
In the proof, we use the quadratic growth property of
\mu
-SC function
F({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
, i.e., for any given
{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle\frac{\mu}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}\leq F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}}))-F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}),\quad\text{ for all }{\mathbf{y}},

(22)

where
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})=\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime}}F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime})
. Using
L_{\Phi}
-smoothness of
{\widetilde{\Phi}}(\cdot)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\mathbb{E}\left\langle\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}),\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\rangle+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\langle\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}),\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\rangle+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}

(using
2
and (
3
))

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}.

(23)

Next,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}))-\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right)\right\|^{2}

(since
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})=\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime}}F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime})
)

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+2\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

(Jensen’s inequality;
L_{f}
-smoothness; Young’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\|^{2}

(Quadratic growth of
\mu
-SC functions (
22
); Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]

(
L_{f}
-smoothness)

\displaystyle=\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i).

(24)

where
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]
.
Further, the term containing
\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\|^{2}
in (
14
) is bounded in
Lemma

B.7
.

substituting the bounds from (
24
), (
14
) and
Lemma

B.7
into (
23
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left[\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\left[\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\right)2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n\sigma_{L}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\right)2(\max_{i}w_{i})\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\beta_{L}^{2}\left[2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\beta_{L}^{2}4\beta_{G}^{2}\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)+\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-\frac{7\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{16}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{9\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{4\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+\frac{5}{4}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(2(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n-P}{n-1}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right],

where,
the coefficients are simplified, using assumptions on the learning rate
\gamma_{x}^{s}

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\left[(\max_{i}w_{i})\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\right)+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{P}{4n}

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\left[(\max_{i}w_{i})\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\right)+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{P}{32\beta_{G}^{2}n}.

This finishes the proof of.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

B.4
.

We use the client update equations for individual iterates (
12
). To bound
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)
, first we bound a single component term
\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
. For
1\leq k\leq\tau_{i}
,
Using modified variance assumption

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}=[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\left(\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)-\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)+\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left[\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\left(\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)-\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right)\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

(using unbiasedness in
2
)

\displaystyle=[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)-\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}})\right\|^{2}\right)+\left(\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\right)\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right].

(25)

where the last inequality follows from Jensen’s inequality (
Lemma

A.3
). Next, note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}=\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\leq{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-[a^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}(\tau_{i})]^{2},

(26)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)=\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\leq{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}-[a^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}(\tau_{i})],

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\leq\alpha\cdot\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i}),

We define
\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\triangleq{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-[a^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}(\tau_{i})]^{2}
,
\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}\triangleq{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}-[a^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}(\tau_{i})]

for the sake of brevity. Using (
26
), we bound the individual terms in (
25
).

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]\Delta^{(t,j)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}.

(27)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\left(\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\right)\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\left(\sum_{j=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\right)\sum_{j=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\left[L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,j)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}+\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\tau_{i}-2}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\Delta^{(t,j)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}+2\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}.

(28)

Substituting (
27
), (
28
) in (
25
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+2[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}.

(29)

Similarly, we can bound the
{\mathbf{y}}
error

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+2[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}.

(30)

Combining the two bounds in (
29
) and (
30
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\sigma_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+2\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left[[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right].

(31)

Define
A_{m}\triangleq 2L_{f}^{2}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\max_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)
. Rearranging the terms in (
31
), and taking the weighted sum over agents, we get

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}}{1-A_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{1-A_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\left[[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}}{1-A_{m}}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}{1-A_{m}}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}{1-A_{m}}[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right).

(32)

where (
32
) follows from
3
, and we define
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.
We bounded
\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}F\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}
in
Lemma

B.6
. Similarly, we can bound
\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}F\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}
as follows.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}F\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}F\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)-\nabla_{y}F\left({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right)\right\|^{2}

(
\because{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})=\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime}}F({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}^{\prime})
)

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(33)

using
L_{f}
-smoothness and concavity of
F({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
(
Lemma

A.6
). Also, for the choice of
\eta_{x}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}
, we get
A_{m}\leq 1/2
.
Consequently, substituting the two bounds in (
32
), we complete the proof.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

B.5
.

Using
L_{f}
-smoothness (
1
) of
F({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})-\mathbb{E}\left\langle\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}),{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\langle\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}),\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\rangle+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\|^{2}.

(34)

Next, we bound the individual terms in (
34
). Using quadratic growth of
\mu
-SC functions (
Lemma

A.5
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\geq 2\mu\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

Next, we bound
\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}
, using similar reasoning as in (
24
).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}+2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i),

(35)

We can bound
\mathbb{E}\big\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\big\|^{2}
using (
14
) in
Lemma

B.2
to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n(n-P)}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\beta_{L}^{2}n}{P}\left[2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+4\beta_{G}^{2}\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\beta_{L}^{2}n}{P}2\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left[\sigma_{G}^{2}+4\beta_{G}^{2}L_{f}\kappa\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{n(n-P)}{n-1}\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{n(n-P)}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right)\frac{2\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left[4L_{f}\kappa\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2n\sigma_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\frac{2nL_{f}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8n\beta_{G}^{2}L_{f}\kappa}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(36)

Similarly, we can bound
\mathbb{E}\big\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\big\|^{2}
to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\frac{2nL_{f}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\beta_{G}^{2}L_{f}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(37)

Substituting (
35
), (
36
), (
37
) and
Lemma

B.4
in (
34
), and rearranging the terms, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{4n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\left(1+\frac{2\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}\right)\left[\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}n}{P}\left(\frac{2\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}+1\right)\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\right]L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\left(\frac{4\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\kappa}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}+1\right)\frac{\beta_{G}^{2}nL_{f}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}\gamma_{y}^{s}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\frac{4\beta_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\left[\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

(
\because 4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\leq 1,\gamma_{x}^{s}\kappa\leq\gamma_{y}^{s}
)

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

(
\because 8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\leq P
)

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\frac{\beta_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

(38)

where we simplify some coefficients using
4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\leq 1,\gamma_{x}^{s}\leq\kappa\gamma_{y}^{s}
. We rearrange the terms
and use the bound in
Lemma

B.4
to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\left[\frac{n\sigma_{L}^{2}}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2n\sigma_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4L_{f}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

(39)

Next, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})+{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(40)

Substituting the bound from
Lemma

B.4
into
Lemma

B.3
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right]
can be bounded as follows.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{3\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{8}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{\Phi}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{5\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{2\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(2(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n-P}{n-1}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{5}{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right].

(41)

where, we use
20L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}
,
40L_{f}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\mu}
.
Next, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
. Again, using
L_{f}
-smoothness of
F(\cdot,{\mathbf{y}})
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle-\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}),{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})+\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{C}^{(t)}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

(
L_{f}
-smoothness,
Lemma

A.5
)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{2}\left(1+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(using (
36
),
Lemma

B.4
)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+4\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sigma_{G}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sigma_{G}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}4\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right),

(42)

where the coefficients are simplified since the choices of learning rates ensures that

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{4n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{4n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\frac{4n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu}

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\frac{8n\beta_{G}^{2}}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1

We substitute the bounds from (
41
), (
42
) in (
40
), and subsequently in (
39
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\left[1+\frac{3\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}+\frac{5\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{2\mu}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4L_{f}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{2}\left(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\left[\frac{13\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{8}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}}{2}(L_{\Phi}+L_{f})\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\left[\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+4\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\sigma_{G}^{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\frac{5}{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{2}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(2(\max_{i}w_{i})\frac{n-P}{n-1}+2\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

(43)

Next, we simplify the coefficients of different terms in (

).

•

Coefficient of
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
can be simplified to

\displaystyle(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\left(1+\frac{11\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{2\mu}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4L_{f}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(2\kappa[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 1-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu}{4}.

using
\gamma_{x}^{s}\leq\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}}{11\kappa^{2}}
,
\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\kappa L_{f}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{n}{P}\max\left\{\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}},\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{64}
,
\kappa L_{f}\beta_{G}\eta_{x}^{c}\leq\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
and
L_{f}\beta_{G}\eta_{y}^{c}\leq\frac{1}{16\sqrt{\kappa M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
.

•

Coefficient of
\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}
can be simplified to

\displaystyle\tau_{\text{eff}}^{3}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}}{2}(L_{\Phi}+L_{f})\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}.

(
\because L_{f}\leq L_{\Phi},\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\leq 1
)

•

Coefficient of
\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}
can be simplified to

\displaystyle(1-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu)\frac{13}{8}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\left[\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+4M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}}{48\kappa^{2}}.

(
\frac{\gamma_{x}^{s}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}\leq\frac{1}{49\kappa^{2}}
)

using
\gamma_{x}^{s}\leq\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}}{156\kappa^{2}}
,
\eta_{x}^{c}L_{f}\beta_{G}\leq\frac{1}{64\kappa\sqrt{M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
and
\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\beta_{G}\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\max\left\{\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i},\beta_{L}\sqrt{\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{40\kappa}
.

•

Coefficient of
