\sigma_{L}^{2}
can be simplified to

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}(L_{\Phi}+L_{f})}{2}\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}\left(4\gamma_{y}^{s}+\frac{5}{2}\gamma_{x}^{s}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{9}{2}\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

•

Coefficient of
\sigma_{G}^{2}
can be simplified to

\displaystyle\frac{2\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}n}{P}\left([\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}+[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}(L_{f}+L_{\Phi})\right)\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\left(8\gamma_{y}^{s}+4\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)+5\gamma_{x}^{s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)+9\gamma_{y}^{s}\tau_{\text{eff}}L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+4\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right).

Finally, substituting these coefficients in (
43
), summing over
t=0,\ldots,T-1
and rearranging the terms, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu}\left[\frac{{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)})-F({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)},{\mathbf{y}}^{(0)})}{T}-\frac{\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(T)})-F({\mathbf{x}}^{(T)},{\mathbf{y}}^{(T)})\right)}{T}\right]+\frac{1}{12\mu\kappa^{2}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{8\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{\Phi}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}\mu}\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+18\kappa L_{f}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{8\tau_{\text{eff}}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\kappa}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+6\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\kappa\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right],

(44)

which concludes the proof.
∎

B.4
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma B.6
.

If the local client function
f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
4
(
L_{f}
-smoothness and
\mu
-strong concavity in
{\mathbf{y}}
), then the function
F

satisfies

\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}\big\|^{2}\leq 2\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\big\|^{2}+\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\right]

Proof.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}F\left({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}\right)-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})-{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}

(
L_{f}
-smoothness (
1
))

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\right].

(
4
)

∎

Lemma B.7
.

If the local client function
f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
and
4
, then the iterates
\{{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\}_{i,(t,k)}
generated by
Algorithm

1
satisfy

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+4\beta_{G}^{2}\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)+\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\pm\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

(
L_{f}
-smoothness)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]

(
3
)

Using
Lemma

B.6
gives the result.
∎

Lemma B.8
.

If the local client function
f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
and
4
, then the iterates
\{{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\}_{i,(t,k)}
generated by
Algorithm

1
satisfy

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\sigma_{G}^{2}+2\beta_{G}^{2}L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)\right].

Proof.

Following closely the proof of
Lemma

B.7
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\pm\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

(
L_{f}
-smoothness)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\beta_{G}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right]

(
3
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\sigma_{G}^{2}+2\beta_{G}^{2}L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)\right].

where the final inequality follows from smoothness and concavity of
F
in
{\mathbf{y}}
.
∎

B.5
Convergence under Polyak Łojasiewicz (PL) Condition

In case the global function satisfies
5
, the results in this section follow with minor modifications. The crucial difference is that
Lemma

A.6
no longer holds.
Lemma

B.2
and
Lemma

B.3
follow exactly. The statement of
Lemma

B.4
needs some modification, since we use
Lemma

A.6
in the proof.

Lemma B.9
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
5
, and the stochastic oracles for the local functions satisfy
2
.
Under the conditions of
Lemma

B.9
, the iterates
\{{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t)}}\}
generated by
Algorithm

1
satisfy

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{p_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\leq 2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}p_{i}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\sigma_{G}^{2}

\displaystyle+8L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+8{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\kappa}L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\left(2[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right],

where
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.

The bound in
Lemma

B.8
also changes to

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left(\max_{i}\frac{p_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\left[\sigma_{G}^{2}+2\beta_{G}^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\kappa}L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left({\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)\right].

The same bound in
Lemma

B.5
holds, but with more stringent conditions on learning rates, namely
\eta_{y}^{c}L_{f}\beta_{G}\leq\frac{1}{16\kappa\sqrt{M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}}}
and
\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\kappa L_{f}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{1}{P}\max\left\{\kappa\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}},1\right\}\leq\frac{1}{64}
. Consequently, the bounds in
Theorem

1
hold, under slightly more stringent conditions on the learning rates.

Appendix C
Convergence of Fed-Norm-SGDA+ for Nonconvex Concave Functions (
Theorem

2
)

We organize this section as follows. First, in
Section

C.1
we present some intermediate results, which we use in the proof of
Theorem

2
. Next, in
Section

C.2
, we present the proof of
Theorem

2
, which is followed by the proofs of the intermediate results in
Section

C.3
.
Finally, we discuss the extension of our results to nonconvex-one-point-concave functions in
Section

C.4
.

The problem we solve is

\displaystyle\min_{{\mathbf{x}}}\max_{{\mathbf{y}}}\left\{{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})\right\}.

We define
{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\max_{{\mathbf{y}}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
and
{\widetilde{{\mathbf{y}}}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})\in\argmax_{{\mathbf{y}}}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}})
. Since
{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}},\cdot)
is no longer strongly concave,
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})
need not be unique. In
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ , the client updates are given by

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}={\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-\eta_{x}^{c}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,j)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}),

(45)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}={\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}+\eta_{y}^{c}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,j)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,j)}}),

where
1\leq k\leq\tau_{i}
. The server updates are given by

{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}={\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}},\qquad{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}={\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}},

(46)

where
{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}}}},{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}
are defined in (
3
). The normalized (stochastic) gradient vectors are defined as

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right);\quad{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{x}f_{i}\left({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right),

(47)

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}};{\xi_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right);\quad{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}=\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\nabla_{y}f_{i}\left({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right).

C.1
Intermediate Lemmas

As discussed in
Section

5.2
, we analyze the convergence of the smoothed envelope function
{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}
. We begin with a bound on the one-step decay of this function.

Lemma C.1

(One-step decay of Smoothed-Envelope)
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
6
, and
7
.
Then, the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ satisfy

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right]+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\left\{L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\right\}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{8}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2},

where
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)=\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\|^{2}+\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\|^{2}\right]
is the drift of client
i\in[n]
, at the
k
-th local step of epoch
t
.

Between two successive synchronization time instants (for example,
t,t+1
), the clients drift apart due to local descent/ascent steps, resulting in the
\{\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)\}_{i,k}
terms. Also,
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
quantifies the error of the inner maximization. In the subsequent lemmas, we bound both these error terms.

Lemma C.2

(Consensus Error)
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
3
,
6
, and
7
. The stochastic oracles for the local
functions satisfy
2
.
Further, in
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+, we choose the client learning rate
\eta_{y}^{c}
such that
\eta_{y}^{c}\leq\frac{1}{2L_{f}(\max_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1})\sqrt{2\max\{1,\beta_{L}^{2}\}}}
.
Then, the iterates
\{{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t)}}\}
generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ satisfy

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+8[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right],

where
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2})
.

Note that consensus error depends on the difference
\mathbb{E}[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})]
. This is different from the term
\mathbb{E}[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})]
in
Lemma

C.1
. Since in
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ , the
{\mathbf{x}}
-component stays fixed at
{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}
for
S
communication rounds while updating
{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}
, the difference

\displaystyle\sum_{t=kS}^{(k+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

can be interpreted as the optimization error, when maximizing the concave function
F({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},\cdot)
over
S
communication rounds. Next, we bound this error. The following result essentially extends the analysis of FedNova (
Wang et al. 2020
) to concave maximization (analogously, convex minimization) problems. We also generalize the corresponding analyses in
Khaled et al. 2020
;
Koloskova et al. 2020
to heterogeneous local updates.

Lemma C.3

(Local SG updates for Concave Maximization)
.

Suppose the local functions satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
and
6
. Further, let
\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\leq R
for all
t
. We run
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ with client step-size
\eta_{y}^{c}
such that
64[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}L_{f}^{2}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{n}{P}\leq 1
. Further, the server step-size
\gamma_{y}^{s}
satisfies

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\max\{\beta_{G}^{2},1\}\max\left\{\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}},\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8},

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\max\left\{\frac{P-1}{n-1},\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}.

Then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4L_{f}([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2})\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}(G_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right],

where
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.

Remark 8
.

It is worth noting that the proof of
Lemma

C.3
does not require global concavity of local functions. Rather, given
{\mathbf{x}}
, we only need concavity of local functions
\{f_{i}\}
at some point
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})
. This is precisely the one-point-concavity assumption (
8
) discussed earlier in
Deng & Mahdavi 2021
;
Sharma et al. 2022
. Therefore,
Lemma

C.3
for a much larger class of functions. Further, the bound in
Lemma

C.3
improves the corresponding bounds derived in existing work. As we discuss in
Section

C.4
, this helps us achieve improve complexity results for nonconvex-one-point-concave (NC-1PC) functions.

Next, we bound the difference
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
.

Lemma C.4
.

Suppose the local functions satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
7
.
Then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=0}^{T/S-1}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)}.

C.2
Proof of
Theorem

2

For the sake of completeness, we first state the full statement of
Theorem

2
here.

Theorem 4
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
6
,
7
. Further, let
\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\leq R
for all
t
. We run
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ with client step-size
\eta_{y}^{c}
such that
64[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}L_{f}^{2}\max\{\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{n}{P},1\}\leq 1
. Further, the server step-size
\gamma_{y}^{s}
satisfies

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\max\{\beta_{G}^{2},1\}\max\left\{\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}},\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8},

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\max\left\{\frac{P-1}{n-1},\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}.

Then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\left[\frac{A_{w}}{P\tau_{\text{eff}}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+F_{w}\right)\right]\right)

(48)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{A_{w}}{P\tau_{\text{eff}}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+F_{w}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{L_{f}R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}}{P}\left(A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\tau_{\text{eff}}\frac{n-P}{n-1}E_{w}+B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}\right)\right)\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D\left(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right]\right),

where
{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\min_{{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{\prime})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}
is the envelope function,
\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}\triangleq{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}_{0})-\min_{\mathbf{x}}{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})
,
A_{w}\triangleq n\tau_{\text{eff}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}
,

B_{w}\triangleq n\tau_{\text{eff}}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}
,

E_{w}\triangleq n\max_{i}w_{i}
,

C_{w}\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}({\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-[\alpha^{(t,\tau_{i}-1)}_{i}]^{2})
, and

D\triangleq\max_{i}(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2})
,
F_{w}\triangleq\frac{n(n-P)}{P(n-1)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}
.
With the following parameter values:

\eta_{x}^{c}=\eta_{y}^{c}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L_{f}\bar{\tau}T^{3/8}}\right),\gamma_{x}^{s}=\Theta\left(\frac{P^{1/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}T)^{3/4}}\right),\quad\gamma_{y}^{s}=\Theta\left(\frac{P^{3/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}T)^{1/4}}\right),\quad S=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{T}{\tau_{\text{eff}}P}}\right),

where
\bar{\tau}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tau_{i}
, we can further simplify to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\bar{\tau}/\tau_{\text{eff}})^{1/4}}{(\bar{\tau}PT)^{1/4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\tau_{\text{eff}}P)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}\right)}_{\text{Error with full synchronization}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\cdot\frac{E_{w}}{PT}\right)^{1/4}\right)}_{\text{Partial participation error}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})}{\bar{\tau}^{2}T^{3/4}}\right)}_{\text{Error due to local updates}}.

Proof.

We sum the bound in
Lemma

C.1
over
t=0
to
T-1
and rearrange the terms to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+16L_{f}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+\frac{16}{T}\sum_{s=0}^{T/S-1}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(0)})-{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(T)})\right]}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+32\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+64L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

(From
Lemma

C.2
)

\displaystyle\quad+128[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=0}^{T/S-1}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+16L_{f}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+8\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

(where
\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}\triangleq{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}_{0})-\min_{\mathbf{x}}{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})
)

\displaystyle\quad+32\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+32\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)}

(From
Lemma

C.4
)

\displaystyle\quad+18L_{f}\left[\frac{4R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\frac{n}{P}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right)\right]

(From
Lemma

C.3
; using
A_{m}\leq\min\{\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{16\beta_{G}^{2}}\}
)

\displaystyle\quad+72[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\right].

(49)

We can simplify the notation using the constants
A_{w}\triangleq n\tau_{\text{eff}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}
,
B_{w}\triangleq\tau_{\text{eff}}n\left(\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)
,
E_{w}\triangleq n\max_{i}w_{i}
,
C_{w}\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}
,
D\triangleq M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}
,
F_{w}\triangleq\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}

and drop the numerical constants, for simplicity, to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\left[\frac{A_{w}}{P\tau_{\text{eff}}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+\frac{(n-P)}{(n-1)}F_{w}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{A_{w}}{P\tau_{\text{eff}}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+\frac{(n-P)}{(n-1)}F_{w}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{f}R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}}{P}\left(A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\tau_{\text{eff}}\frac{n-P}{n-1}E_{w}+B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}\right)\right)+\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D\left(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\mathcal{I}_{1}^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}+L_{f}\left[\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\mathcal{I}_{1}+\frac{R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right],

(50)

where in (
50
), to simplify notation, we have defined
\mathcal{I}_{1}\triangleq\sqrt{\frac{A_{w}}{P\tau_{\text{eff}}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}+\frac{(n-P)}{(n-1)}F_{w}\right)}
,
\mathcal{I}_{2}\triangleq A_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+(B_{w}\beta_{L}^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\frac{n-P}{n-1}E_{w})\sigma_{G}^{2}
.

Next, we optimize the algorithm parameters
S,\gamma_{x}^{s},\gamma_{y}^{s},\eta_{y}^{c},\eta_{y}^{c}
to achieve a tight bound on (
50
). If
R=0
, we let
S=1
. Else, let
S=\sqrt{\frac{R}{\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}\gamma_{x}^{s}\gamma_{y}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}}}
. Substituting this in (
50
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\mathcal{I}_{1}^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}+L_{f}\sqrt{\frac{R\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right],

(51)

Next, we focus on the terms in (
51
) containing
\gamma_{y}^{s}
:
L_{f}\left[\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}+\sqrt{\frac{R\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}}}\right]
. To optimize these, we choose
\gamma_{y}^{s}=\left(\frac{P}{2\mathcal{I}_{2}}\right)^{2/3}\left(R\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}\right)^{1/3}
. Substituting in (
51
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\mathcal{I}_{1}^{2}+L_{f}\left(\frac{\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}R\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}\right)^{1/3}

\displaystyle\quad+\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right],

(52)

Finally, we focus on the terms in (
52
) containing
\gamma_{x}^{s}
:
\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+L_{f}\left(\frac{\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}R\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}\right)^{1/3}
. We ignore the higher order linear term. With
\gamma_{x}^{s}=\left(\frac{3\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}L_{f}T}\right)^{3/4}\left(\frac{\mathcal{I}_{2}}{P}RG_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathcal{I}_{1}\right)^{-1/4}
,
and absorbing numerical constants inside
\mathcal{O}(\cdot)
we get,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\left(\mathcal{I}_{1}\mathcal{I}_{2}RG_{{\mathbf{x}}}\right)^{1/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}PT)^{1/4}}+\frac{(\tau_{\text{eff}}P)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}\left(\mathcal{I}_{1}\mathcal{I}_{2}RG_{{\mathbf{x}}}\right)^{-1/4}\mathcal{I}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right],

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\left(\mathcal{I}_{1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\right)^{1/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}PT)^{1/4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\tau_{\text{eff}}P)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}\mathcal{I}_{2}^{3/4}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right]\right),

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\bar{\tau}/\tau_{\text{eff}})^{1/4}}{(\bar{\tau}PT)^{1/4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\left(\frac{n(n-P)}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}\right)^{1/4}\sigma_{G}}{(PT)^{1/4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\tau_{\text{eff}}P)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{O}\left(\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right]\right),

(53)

where in (
53
), we have shown dependence only on
\tau,n,P,T
. Lastly, we specify the algorithm parameters in terms of
n,T,\tau_{\text{eff}},\bar{\tau}
.

\gamma_{x}^{s}=\Theta\left(\frac{P^{1/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}T)^{3/4}}\right),\quad\gamma_{y}^{s}=\Theta\left(\frac{P^{3/4}}{(\tau_{\text{eff}}T)^{1/4}}\right),\quad S=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{T}{\tau_{\text{eff}}P}}\right).

Finally, choosing the client learning rates
\eta_{x}^{c}=\eta_{y}^{c}=\frac{1}{L_{f}\bar{\tau}T^{3/8}}
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\bar{\tau}/\tau_{\text{eff}})^{1/4}}{(\bar{\tau}PT)^{1/4}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(PT)^{1/4}}\left(\frac{n(n-P)}{(n-1)}\max_{i}w_{i}\right)^{1/4}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(\tau_{\text{eff}}P)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{C_{w}\sigma_{L}^{2}+D(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})}{\bar{\tau}^{2}T^{3/4}}\right).

∎

Convergence in terms of
F

Proof of
2.1
.

Following
Lin et al. 2020a
, we define

\displaystyle\begin{matrix}{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\min_{{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}}\left\{{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{\prime})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\right\};\qquad&\qquad\widetilde{{\mathbf{x}}}\triangleq\argmin_{{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}}\left\{{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}}^{\prime})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\right\},\\
\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\triangleq\min_{{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}}\left\{\Phi({\mathbf{x}}^{\prime})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\right\};\qquad&\qquad\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}\triangleq\argmin_{{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}}\left\{\Phi({\mathbf{x}}^{\prime})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\right\}.\end{matrix}

(54)

Also, it follows from Lemma 2.2 in
Davis & Drusvyatskiy 2019
that
\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})=2L_{f}({\mathbf{x}}-\widetilde{{\mathbf{x}}})
and
\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})=2L_{f}({\mathbf{x}}-\bar{{\mathbf{x}}})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=8L_{f}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{{\mathbf{x}}}-\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

Consequently, we obtain

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left[\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

(55)

where
\widetilde{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)},\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}
follow the same definition as in (
54
), with
{\mathbf{x}}
replaced with
{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}
.
∎

Proof of
2.2
.

If clients are weighted equally (
w_{i}=p_{i}=1/n
for all
i
), with each carrying out
\tau
steps of local SGDA+, then (
7
) reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\big\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(\sync\selclients T)^{1/4}}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\sync\selclients)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}$}}\Big)+\mathcal{O}\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\localvar^2 + \sync(G_{\bx}^2 + \hetero^2)}{\sync T^{3/4}}$}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\Big(\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\numclients- \selclients}{\numclients-1}$}}\cdot\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\selclients T }$}}\Big)^{1/4}\Big).

•

For full client participation, this reduces to

\displaystyle\min_{t\in[T]}\mathbb{E}\big\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\big\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(\sync\numclients T)^{1/4}}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\sync\numclients)^{1/4}}{T^{3/4}}$}}\Big).

To reach an
\epsilon
-stationary point, assuming
n\tau\leq T
, the per-client gradient complexity is
T\tau=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{n\epsilon^{8}}\right)
. Since
\tau\leq T/n
, the minimum number of communication rounds required is
T=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}}\right)
.

•

For partial participation,
\mathcal{O}\Big(\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\numclients- \selclients}{\numclients-1}$}}\cdot\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\selclients T }$}}\Big)^{1/4}\Big)
is the dominant term, and we do not get any convergence benefit of multiple local updates. Consequently, per-gradient client complexity and number of communication rounds are both
T\tau=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{P\epsilon^{8}}\right)
, for
\tau=\mathcal{O}(1)
. However, if the data across clients comes from identical distributions (
\sigma_{G}=0
), then we recover
per-client gradient complexity of
\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{P\epsilon^{8}}\right)
, and number of communication rounds
=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}}\right)
.

∎

Special Cases

•

Centralized, deterministic case
(\sigma_{L}=\sigma_{G}=0,\beta_{G}=1,\tau_{\text{eff}}=n=1)
: in this case
A_{w}=B_{w}=1,C_{w}=D=0
. Also,
\mathcal{I}_{1}=G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\sqrt{\beta_{L}^{2}+1},\mathcal{I}_{2}=0
. The bound in (
48
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left[(\beta_{L}^{2}+1)+(S-1)\sqrt{\beta_{L}^{2}+1}\right]+\frac{L_{f}R}{\gamma_{y}^{s}S}\right).

(56)

For
\beta_{L}=0
, (
56
) yields the convergence result in
Lin et al. 2020a
.

•

Single node, stochastic case
(\sigma_{G}=0,\beta_{G}=1,\tau_{\text{eff}}=n=1)
: in this case
A_{w}=B_{w}=1,C_{w}=D=0
. Also,
\mathcal{I}_{1}=\sqrt{\sigma_{L}^{2}+(\beta_{L}^{2}+1)G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}},\mathcal{I}_{2}=\sigma_{L}^{2}
. The bound in (
48
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}(\sigma_{L}^{2}+(\beta_{L}^{2}+1)G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2})+\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\sigma_{L}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta\left(L_{f}\left[\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\sigma_{L}^{2}+(\beta_{L}^{2}+1)G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}}+\frac{R}{\gamma_{y}^{s}S}\right]\right).

(57)

Again, for
\beta_{L}=0
, (
57
) yields the convergence result in
Lin et al. 2020a
.

•

Multiple equally weighted
(w_{i}=1/n,\forall\ i\in[n])
clients, full client participation, stochastic case with synchronous client updates
(\tau_{\text{eff}}=1)
: in this case
A_{w}=1,B_{w}=1,C_{w}=D=0,0
. The bound in (
48
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\Theta\left(\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\left(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}}{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\Theta\left(\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}}{n}(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\beta_{L}^{2})+\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}}{n}}+\frac{RL_{f}}{\gamma_{y}^{s}S}\right),

(58)

Note that unlike existing analyses of synchronous update algorithms
Woodworth et al. 2020
;
Yun et al. 2022
;
Sharma et al. 2022
, the bound in (
58
) depends on the inter-client heterogeneity
\sigma_{G}^{2}
. This is due to the more general noise assumption (
2
). In the existing works,
\beta_{L}^{2}
is assumed zero, in which case, the bound in (
58
) is also independent of
\sigma_{G}^{2}
. See
Section

A.2
for a more detailed explanation.

•

Multiple, equally weighted
(w_{i}=1/n,\forall\ i\in[n])
clients, full client participation, multiple, but equal number of client updates
(\tau_{i}=\tau_{\text{eff}}=\tau,\forall\ i\in[n])
. In this case
A_{w}=B_{w}=1,C_{w}=\tau-1,D=(\tau-1)(\tau-1+\beta_{L}^{2})
. The bound in (
48
) then reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}\leq\Theta\left(\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{{\widetilde{\Phi}}}}{\tau\gamma_{x}^{s}T}+\tau\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\left(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}}{n\tau}\right)+\frac{\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2})}{n}\right)

(59)

\displaystyle+\Theta\left(L_{f}\left[\tau\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}}{n\tau}}+\frac{R}{\tau\gamma_{y}^{s}S}\right]+(\tau-1)\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}+(\tau-1+\beta_{L}^{2})(G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right]\right).

For
\beta_{L}=\beta_{G}=0
, this setting reduces to the one considered in
Sharma et al. 2022
. However, as stated earlier, our bound on the local update error is tighter.

C.3
Proofs of the Intermediate Lemmas

Proof of
Lemma

C.1
.

Using the definition in (
54
)
\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}=\argmin_{\mathbf{x}}{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
. Also, note that

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})

\displaystyle\leq{\widetilde{\Phi}}(\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)})+L_{f}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}.

(60)

Using the
{\mathbf{x}}
updates in (
46
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}}\right\|^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\langle\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\langle\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\rangle+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{L_{f}}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\left(\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})-\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t)}}\tilde{w}_{i}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

(61)

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}+2L_{f}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left\langle\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\rangle\right],

(62)

where (
62
) follows from
L_{f}
-smoothness (
1
) and Jensen’s inequality.
From (
19
), (
20
), we can bound
\mathbb{E}\big\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t^{\prime})}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x},i}^{(t^{\prime})}\big\|^{2}
as follows.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t^{\prime})}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x},i}^{(t^{\prime})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{x}f_{i}({\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{n}{P}\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{x}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+\frac{n(P-1)}{P(n-1)}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+\frac{n(n-P)}{P(n-1)}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2},

(63)

where the final inequality by using
7
. Next, we bound the inner product term in (
62
). Using
L_{f}
-smoothness of
F
(
1
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\langle\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\rangle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}(\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}(\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)})+L_{f}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\frac{L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})+L_{f}\left\|{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\frac{L_{f}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

(by definition of
\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}
)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right].

(64)

Substituting the bounds from (
62
) and (
64
) into (
60
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t+1)}})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}(\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)})+L_{f}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}L_{f}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\bar{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right]+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{x}^{s}]^{2}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\left\{L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\right\}-\frac{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}}{8}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2},

where we use
\nabla{\widetilde{\Phi}}_{1/2L_{f}}({\mathbf{x}})=2L_{f}({\mathbf{x}}-\bar{{\mathbf{x}}})
from (
54
).
∎

Proof of
Lemma

C.2
.

We use the client update equations for individual iterates in (
45
). To bound
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)
, first we bound the
{\mathbf{x}}
-error
\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
.
Starting from (
25
), using
7
, for
1\leq k\leq\tau_{i}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\left[\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}[a_{i}^{(j)}(k)]^{2}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+\left(\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)\right)\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}a_{i}^{(j)}(k)G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq[\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\right],

(65)

where we use (
26
).
Next, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}
, using the bound from (
30
), to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\quad+2[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}.

(66)

Compared to (
30
), the difference is the presence of
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)
in (
66
), rather than
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)
.
Taking a weighted sum over agents in (
66
), we get

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq 2[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left(\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)\right].

(67)

where, we choose
\eta_{y}^{c}
such that
A_{m}\triangleq 2L_{f}^{2}[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\max_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}+\beta_{L}^{2}\alpha\right)\leq\frac{1}{2}
, and define
M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\triangleq\max_{i}\left(\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{1}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.
Next, it follows from
L_{f}
-smoothness (
1
) and
Lemma

A.7
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}\left({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

Subsequently, combining (
65
) and (
67
), we get

\displaystyle L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)L_{f}^{2}\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+8[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}^{3}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

which finishes the proof.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

C.3
.

We define
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\in\argmax_{\mathbf{y}}{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}})
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq:server_update_alg_NC_C_minimax_PCP}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\|^{2}+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left\langle{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}),\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\rangle.

(68)

\mathbb{E}\left\|{{\mathbf{g}}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\|^{2}
is bounded in (
37
). We only need to further bound
\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}
which appears in (
37
).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\left(\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})-\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+2\left\|\nabla_{y}{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right\|^{2}

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{f}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)+4L_{f}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(69)

Next, we bound the third term in (
68
).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\langle{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}),\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}{\mathbf{h}^{(t)}_{{\mathbf{y}},i}}\right\rangle=\mathbb{E}\left\langle{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}),\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}},\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}),\nabla_{y}f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\mathbb{E}\Bigg[f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}}\right\|^{2}

(
L_{f}
-smoothness)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}_{i}^{(t,k)}})-f_{i}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}))\Bigg]

(Concavity in
{\mathbf{y}}
)

\displaystyle=\frac{L_{f}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}(i)-\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(70)

Substituting (
37
), (
69
), (
70
) in (
68
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}\left[\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\left(1-2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\beta_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]\right)\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\left[1+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)\right]\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}[a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})]\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i),

(71)

since
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{y}}}\leq\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}
. Using the bound on
\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}
from
Lemma

C.2
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\sum_{k=0}^{\tau_{i}-1}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})\Delta^{(t,k)}_{{\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{y}}}(i)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\right)\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+4M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\left([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+8[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}L_{f}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}\beta_{G}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(72)

We substitute (
72
) in (
71
), and simplify the terms using the choice of
\gamma_{y}^{s},\eta_{y}^{c}
to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t+1)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}+\tau_{\text{eff}}^{2}[\gamma_{y}^{s}]^{2}\left[\frac{\sigma_{L}^{2}n}{P}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+\frac{2\sigma_{G}^{2}n}{P}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+4\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2})\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}(G_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right].

using
\gamma_{y}^{s},\eta_{y}^{c}
that satisfy

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i,k}\frac{w_{i}a_{i}^{(k)}(\tau_{i})}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1,

\displaystyle 2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\beta_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4},

\displaystyle 2L_{f}\frac{n}{P}\left[8[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2}M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}L_{f}\beta_{G}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}

Then the coefficient of
\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]
can we bounded by
-\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}
.
Consequently, by rearranging the terms and summing over
t
, we get the result.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{y}}^{sS}-{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})\right\|^{2}}{\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}S}+\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{y}^{s}\frac{n}{P}\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{{2}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}+2\sigma_{G}^{2}\left(\frac{n-P}{n-1}\max_{i}w_{i}+\beta_{L}^{2}\max_{i}\frac{w_{i}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+4L_{f}([\eta_{x}^{c}]^{2}+[\eta_{y}^{c}]^{2})\left[\sigma_{L}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i,-1}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2M_{\mathbf{a}_{-1}}(G_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+\sigma_{G}^{2})\right].

∎

Proof of
Lemma

C.4
.

Let
t=sS,sS+1,\dots,(s+1)S-1
, where
k
is a positive integer. Let
{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}
is the latest snapshot iterate for the
{\mathbf{y}}
-update in
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ . Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}))-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}))+{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}))-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})+{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}))-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}))\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}))-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2G_{{\mathbf{x}}}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}-{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}\right\|+\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right].

(73)

where,
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}(\cdot)\in\argmax_{\mathbf{y}}{\widetilde{F}}(\cdot,{\mathbf{y}})
and (
73
) follows from
G_{{\mathbf{x}}}
-Lipschitz continuity of
F(\cdot,{\mathbf{y}})
(
7
). Next, we see that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\|{\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)}-{\mathbf{x}^{(t)}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:server_update_alg_NC_C_minimax_PCP}}{=}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\|\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\sum_{t^{\prime}=sS}^{t-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t^{\prime})}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x},i}^{(t^{\prime})}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}\sqrt{(S-1)\sum_{t^{\prime}=sS}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}^{(t^{\prime})}}\tilde{w}_{i}{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x},i}^{(t^{\prime})}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)}.

(from (
63
))

Using this bound in (
73
), and summing over
t
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}})-{\widetilde{F}}({\mathbf{x}^{(t)}},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]\leq\frac{1}{S}\sum_{t=sS}^{(s+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\left[{\widetilde{\Phi}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)})-{\widetilde{F}}({\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}}^{(s)},{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\tau_{\text{eff}}\gamma_{x}^{s}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}(S-1)\sqrt{\frac{n}{P}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{w_{i}^{2}{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{{\left\|\boldsymbol{a}_{i}\right\|}_{1}^{2}}\left(\sigma_{L}^{2}+\beta_{L}^{2}G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\right)+G_{{\mathbf{x}}}^{2}\left(\frac{P-1}{n-1}+\frac{n-P}{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{2}\right)}.

Finally, summing
over
s=0
to
T/S-1
we get the result.
∎

C.4
Extending the result for Nonconvex One-Point-Concave (NC-1PC) Functions (
Theorem

3
)

Carefully revisting the proof of
Theorem

2
, we notice that
Lemma

C.1
and
Lemma

C.2
do not rely on the concavity assumption.
Lemma

C.3
does use concavity of local functions
\{f_{i}\}
. However, it is only needed to derive (
70
). Further, this only requires concavity of local functions at a global point
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}(\widehat{{\mathbf{x}}}^{(s)})
. Therefore, as mentioned earlier in
Remark

8
, it holds even for NC-1PC functions. This is an independent result in itself, since we have extended the existing convergence result of local stochastic gradient method for convex minimization (concave maximization) problems, to a much more general one-point-convex minimization (or one-point-convex maximization) problem. Therefore, we restate it here for the more general case.

Lemma C.5

(Local SG updates for One-Point-Concave Maximization)
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
7
. Suppose for all
{\mathbf{x}}
, all the
f_{i}
’s satisfy
8
at a common global minimizer
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})
, and that
\big\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\big\|^{2}\leq R
for all
t
. If we run
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ with same conditions on the client and server step-sizes
\eta_{y}^{c},\gamma_{y}^{s}
respectively, as in
Lemma

C.3
, then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ also satisfy the bound in
Lemma

C.3
.

Next,
Lemma

C.4
also holds irrespective of concavity. Therefore, the resulting convergence result in
Theorem

2
for nonconvex-concave minimax problems holds for a much larger class of functions. We restate the modified theorem statement briefly.

Theorem
.

Suppose the local loss functions
\{f_{i}\}
satisfy Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
7
. Suppose for all
{\mathbf{x}}
, all the
f_{i}
’s satisfy
8
at a common global minimizer
{\mathbf{y}}^{*}({\mathbf{x}})
, and that
\big\|{\mathbf{y}^{(t)}}\big\|^{2}\leq R
for all
t
.
If we run
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ with the same conditions on the client and server step-sizes
\eta_{y}^{c},\gamma_{y}^{s}
respectively, as in
Theorem

4
, then the iterates generated by
Algorithm

1
-Fed-Norm-SGDA+ also satisfy the bound in
Theorem

4
.

Remark 9
.

Again, choosing client weights
\{w_{i}\}
the same as in the original global objective
\{p_{i}\}
, we get convergence in terms of the original objective
F
.

Appendix D
Additional Experiments

For communicating parameters and related information amongst the clients, ethernet connections were used.
Our algorithm was implemented using parallel training tools in PyTorch 1.0.0 and Python 3.6.3.

For both robust NN Training and fair classification experiments, we use batch-size of
32
in all the algorithms. Momentum parameter
0.9
is used only in Momentum Local SGDA(+).

Robust NN Training

Here we further explore performance of Fed-Norm-SGDA+ on the robust NN training problem. We use VGG-11 model to classify CIFAR10 dataset. In
Figure

6
, we demonstrate the effect of increasing data heterogeneity across clients, whle in
Figure

7
we show the advantage of using multiple clients for the federated minimax problem. With
k
-fold increase in
n
, we observe an almost
k
-fold drop in the number of communication rounds needed to reach a target test accuracy (
70\%
here.).

Figure 6:
Effect of inter-client data heterogeneity (quantified by
\alpha
) on the performance of Fed-Norm-SGDA+.

Figure 7:
Effect of increasing client-set on the performance of Fed-Norm-SGDA+ in a robust NN training task.

Fair Classification

We also demonstrate the impact of partial client participation in the fair classification problem.
Figure

8
complements
Figure

8
in the main paper, evaluating fairness of a VGG11 model on CIFAR10 dataset. We have plotted the test accuracy of the model over the worst distribution. With an increasing number of participating clients, the performance consistently improves.

Figure 8:
Effect of partial client participation on the performance of Fed-Norm-SGDA in a fair image classification task.
